They had to promise their daughter immortality.<br><br>It took a small fortune, but eventually they found a vampire who was willing to turn her. Now a fledgling vampire, Mina is quickly discovering that the clout she held as a human has no weight in vampire high society...and while at first that annoyed her, if there is one thing Mina has a knack for, it's learning the rules so she can subvert them.  She will be the Viscount of New York, the other vampires just don't know it yet."
		"hero_punkgoat_lore"			"Billy is a white hot ball of rage, furious at the very notion of...something.  Maybe it's the cops. Maybe it's magic regulations. Maybe it's the service at the gyro place downtown.  (Not the good one.  The other one.)<br><br>Don't ask him how he got the goat head."
		"hero_doorman_lore"				"He's proper, he's polite, but the humble Doorman of The Baroness Hotel is more than meets the eye.<br><br>While he had only planned to experience life on this plane for a few weeks, The Doorman quickly discovered that the New York service industry was a great way to see the strange demands that please humans."
		"hero_familiar_lore"			"An accidental stowaway from the world of dreams, Rem has found himself stranded in the city that never sleeps.  Driven by his curious nature, Rem and his helpful friends have been seen wandering across the five boroughs in search of a way back home.<br><br>So if you ever find yourself dozing off while taking the Blue line home from work, maybe you've just had an encounter with one of the Cursed Apple's newest denizens."
		"hero_necro_lore"			"The daughter of high-power defense attorneys, Darcy Graves grew up in a home where every conversation was a debate, argument, or negotiation. It was exhausting. But while her parents were often too busy meeting with clients or working on cases to bond with her, the dead were always there to listen.<br><br>Graves enjoyed her time in the cemetery.  It was a quiet place to calm her thoughts and talk through her problems...but one day her one-sided conversations started to change.<br><br>It began with a faint whisper.  A woman's voice that seemed to respond to Graves' complaints.  Intrigued, the teenager listened...and followed the voice's instructions until it guided her to a lead-lined box buried in Van Cortlandt Park.  But while most high school students would freak out if they were to unearth a severed lich hand, Graves was certainly not most teens.  The hand now free of its prison, the lich's voice grew stronger... It had spent years looking to find a student she could pass her knowledge to, and Graves was eager to learn.<br><br>Now a fledgling necromancer, Darcy balances not only her homework at Blackmore Academy...but the demands of graveyard denizens, each of whom have their own unfinished business that they seek her aid in resolving."
		"hero_werewolf_lore"					"Lilah Silver is a mess.  She drinks too much, she smokes too much, and the less is said about her personal life the better.   But despite Silver's flaws she has managed to carve out a career as a successful bounty hunter, and while she would like to think that her successes are solely because she has incredible intuition, the fact that she's a werewolf doesn't hurt."
		"hero_priest_lore"				"The Venators of St. Benedict are a Vatican-sanctioned order whose members are deployed to regions with a high concentration of supernatural predators. Few in number, their modus operandi is to observe, identify, and exterminate. They are methodical, they are precise, and once they identify a threat to the flock they are relentless.<br><br>But even amongst those who are called The Sword Of St. Benedict, one stands apart from the others. A man whose hardened heart has allowed him to pay terrible prices in the name of the greater good... and he has once again been dispatched to New York."
		"hero_unicorn_lore"			"Even in a world of magic, some things are still rare and unusual. So what is a unicorn to do? Announce her presence to the world and potentially have poachers looking to saw off her horn? Or hide in plain sight at a Coney Island Side Show where most folk would assume her appearance was just part of an act?<br><br>Over the years \"The Dazzling Celeste\" has grown into one of the more popular attractions on Coney, and while the applause from a cheering audience is appreciated; what keeps her waking up in the morning is the found family she's made at work.<br><br>Keruba the Mermaid.<br><br>Blockhead Lenny.<br><br>Chickenwing.<br><br>Ted.<br><br>These were her people. So when she discovered that one of her own sold part of their soul in order to cover a gambling debt, Celeste knew it was time to step out of the shadows and do whatever it takes to get it back. Is revealing her power to The Patrons a risk? Yes. But that's tomorrow's problem... her family needs help today."
		"hero_fencer_lore"			"A land of beauty, tradition and conflict; North Ixia is no stranger to brutal political violence. So when Apollo was told that it was for his own safety he was being sent to New York for school, he knew the situation was serious.<br><br>Adjusting to life at Blackmore Academy didn't come easy to Apollo. His school was supposed to be filled with The Cursed Apple's best and brightest but all he saw, with few exceptions, was a sea of aggressive mediocrity. Electing not to waste his time with frivolous things like parties and dances, he instead put his energy on the piste, where he rigorously trains as captain of the Blackmore fencing team.<br><br>However, at the end of the day Apollo does not fret too much on the tedium of life in New York. He knows that eventually the time will come when his father will call on him to come home and he will get to show that he is worthy of wielding his family's ancestral blade."
		"hero_ratking_lore"				"The cryptid ruler of the New York Warrens, The Rat King towers over his subjects in size, strength, and ambition. No longer content with hiding underground, the king has revealed his bold vision for a future where he and his subjects can walk the surface without fear of the pest control vans that roam the streets of New York (or as the rats call them: human death squads)<br><br>Step 1: Take control of Long Island<br>Step 2: Declare Long Island as the new sovereign nation of Ratannia<br>Step 3: Execute every human that refuses to leave this new rat utopia<br><br>But air tight as this plan is, the king of rats didn't want to take chances: and so he's decided to summon the patron to help hedge his bets."
		"hero_baba_lore"						"There are a few things most denizens of The Cursed Apple hope to experience at some point in their lives.<br><br>See a Broadway show.<br>Ice Skate at Rockefeller Center during Christmas season.<br>Get yelled at by the Central Park Pigeon Lady.<br><br>Because Baba is more than just an old woman that feeds spirit pigeons in the park... she's an institution. A force of nature woven into the very lore of New York City. Is it true that she's sat at the same bench every day for the past 50 years, rain or shine? Maybe. Is she REALLY the leader of The Soho Coven? Who's to say. One thing's for sure though... she's a New Yorker.  And she's not going to sit back and watch a bunch of tourists tear apart Manhattan in pursuit of power."
		"hero_chessmaster_lore"			"In a world that moves too fast, Solomon is content to stop and listen. Offering a friendly ear and good counsel, Solomon has found himself sitting across the chess table from tourists, criminals, and titans of industry.  But no matter his opponent, Solomon remains un-phased... because to him, chess is so much more than a game. It's an equalizer. A way for him to connect to people from all walks of life on an even playing field."
		"hero_deadpack_lore"			"\"Ladies and Gentlemen, I'm going to tell you a shocking secret: at Fairfax Industries we know nothing about safety. Now let me tell you something even MORE shocking... that's a good thing! Now in my tenure here I have been shot, stabbed, impaled, defenestrated, blown up, atomized, and decapitated-not once. not twice. not thrice. but quice. That's right, my head has been cut off enough times that I had to use a made up word to quantify it.<br><br>But I put myself through figurative and sometimes literal hell because it's not enough to THINK about how things go wrong, we need to KNOW how things go wrong.<br><br>Now the demonstration you are about to witness is extremely dangerous. So sit back. Put on your safety googles. And bear witness to the miracle of flight courtesy of Fairfax Industries, and the Chimera Mark 1.\"<br><br><span class=\"italics\">- This transcription of Danny's opening remarks at The Military Technology Expo has been provided by Fairfax Industries</span>"
